      111 +                                                              
      112 +## Project Context (Graduation Project)                       
      113 +                                                              
      114 +This is a graduation project. The proposal document defines re
          +quirements and planned features — use these as the source of t
          +ruth for scope decisions.                                     
      115 +                                                              
      116 +### Planned Features Not Yet Implemented                      
      117 +                                                              
      118 +These are specified in the proposal (FR = Functional Requireme
          +nt) and should be built:                                      
      119 +                                                              
      120 +| ID | Feature | Priority |                                   
      121 +|----|---------|---------|                                    
      122 +| FR-11 | **Streak tracking** | Medium — partially done (strea
          +k stored on User, but no UI display) |                        
      123 +| FR-12 | **Motivational quotes** — daily Quran verses or Hadi
          +th on the dashboard | Low |                                   
      124 +| Achievements | Award badges for milestones: completing a Sur
          +ah, completing a Juz, memorizing 100/300/604 pages | Medium | 
      125 +| Arabic text | Display Arabic page text with proper diacritic
          +s (tashkeel) — NFR-07 | Medium |                              
      126 +                                                              
      127 +### Non-Functional Requirements to Keep in Mind               
      128 +                                                              
      129 +- **Performance (NFR-01)**: All pages and core interactions mu
          +st load within 3 seconds.                                     
      130 +- **Browser support (NFR-02)**: Latest Chrome, Firefox, Safari
          +, Edge.                                                       
      131 +- **Responsive design (NFR-03)**: Must work well on desktop, t
          +ablet, and mobile.                                            
      132 +- **Arabic text (NFR-07)**: Arabic Quranic text must render wi
          +th correct tashkeel (diacritics).                             
      133 +                                                              
      134 +### Design Rationale                                          
      135 +                                                              
      136 +The smart review system is grounded in **Ebbinghaus's Forgetti
          +ng Curve** and **spaced repetition** (Pimsleur 1967, Leitner S
          +ystem 1972): pages recently memorized are reviewed frequently;
          + intervals grow as retention strengthens. The current implemen
          +tation approximates this by pulling the oldest-reviewed pages 
          +first (up to 3/day). A more faithful spaced repetition algorit
          +hm (dynamic intervals per page based on review history) is a n
          +atural future enhancement.                                    
      137 +                                                              
      138 +The term **Muraja'ah** (ةعجارم) refers to the Islamic traditio
          +n of systematic Quran revision — the "review" tasks in this ap
          +p are the digital equivalent.

  ---
  Plan

  Phase 7 — UI Polish (~30 min)

  7.1 — Rotating daily quotes (the only real unfinished UI item)

  The Dashboard has one static Sahih Bukhari hadith hardcoded. I'll replace it
  with an array of ~10–15 curated Quran/Hadith quotes and rotate by day-of-year
  so the quote changes daily automatically — no backend needed.

  Files touched: client/src/pages/Dashboard.jsx only.

  ---
  Phase 8 — Deployment (~1.5 hours)

  8.1 — Fix API URL (code change, ~5 min)

  client/src/services/api.js has baseURL: 'http://localhost:5000/api' hardcoded.
   I'll change it to:
  baseURL: import.meta.env.VITE_API_URL || 'http://localhost:5000/api'
  Then create client/.env (for dev) and client/.env.production (for after Render
   deploy).

  8.2 — Deploy backend to Render (~45 min, you do the clicks)

  Render is the standard free option. The steps:
  1. Go to render.com → New Web Service → connect your GitHub repo
  2. Root directory: server, Build command: npm install, Start command: node 
  server.js
  3. Add env vars: MONGODB_URI, JWT_SECRET, NODE_ENV=production
  4. Copy the live URL (e.g. https://quran-tracker-api.onrender.com)

  I'll create a render.yaml file so the config is tracked in git.

  8.3 — Deploy frontend to Vercel (~30 min, you do the clicks)

  1. Go to vercel.com → New Project → connect GitHub repo
  2. Root directory: client, Framework: Vite
  3. Add env var: VITE_API_URL=https://your-render-url.onrender.com/api
  4. Deploy → copy live URL

  I'll create a vercel.json for routing config (needed so React Router works on
  page refresh).

  ---
  Phase 9 — Final Documentation (~1.5 hours, you do this)

  After deployment is live:
  - Screenshots of all 9 pages (Landing, Register, Login, Onboarding 1–4,
  Dashboard active, Dashboard complete, Progress, Settings) + mobile views
  - Update the graduation paper with:
    - Deployment URLs (Render + Vercel)
    - Any feature changes since proposal
    - Screenshots in Appendix C
    - Final proofread

  1 page every 2 days instead of splitting pages.                               
                                                                                
  Bug 3: If user misses a day, don't double next day's work.                    
  Shift the plan and merge missed review pages into next cycle.                 
                                                                                
  === FEATURES (backend logic) ===                                              
                                                                                
  Feature 1: Improve the review/revision system.                                
  - 1-7 Juz memorized → 7-day review cycle                                      
  - 8-14 Juz → 7-day cycle (2 Juz/day)                                          
  - 15-30 Juz → 10-day cycle (2-3 Juz/day)                                      
  - Add review intensity option: Light (14 days),                               
    Standard (10), Strong (7)                                                   
                                                                                
  Feature 2: Off days — add support for 1-2 off days                            
  per week in the user model and plan generation logic.                         
                                                                                
  Feature 3: Hafiz mode — if user memorized all 30 Juz,                         
  switch to review-only mode (2-3 Juz/day).                                     
                                                                                
  === RULES ===                                                                 
  - ONLY backend/logic/controller/model changes                                 
  - Do NOT modify any React components or frontend files                        
  - We will rebuild the entire frontend later with a new design

● Good. I have a complete picture. Here's the full plan.

  ---
  Plan: Backend Logic Changes

  Files touched: 4 backend files only. Zero frontend files.

  ---
  1. server/models/User.js — Add 3 new fields

  Field: planStartDate
  Type: Date
  Default: null
  Purpose: Set at onboarding completion; used for per-day page allocation.
    Fallback: createdAt
  ────────────────────────────────────────
  Field: reviewIntensity
  Type: String enum ['light','standard','strong']
  Default: 'standard'
  Purpose: Controls review cycle length
  ────────────────────────────────────────
  Field: offDays
  Type: [Number] (0=Sun…6=Sat)
  Default: []
  Purpose: Days with no tasks

  ---
  2. Bug 1 — Estimated completion time

  Root cause: The estimate currently lives entirely in the frontend and uses 604
   pages total without subtracting already-memorized pages. Since we can't touch
   the frontend now, the fix is to expose the correct calculation from the
  backend so the rebuilt UI can use it.

  Change: Add GET /api/progress/estimate endpoint (new route in
  progressRoutes.js, new function in progressController.js).

  Query param: ?dailyPages=0.5 (optional — defaults to user's saved
  dailyNewPages).

  Response:
  {
    "remainingPages": 312,
    "dailyPages": 1,
    "estimatedDays": 312,
    "estimatedMonths": 10,
    "adjustedForOffDays": true
  }

  Also add estimatedCompletion to the stats block in GET /api/progress/today so
  any UI can pick it up without an extra request.

  ---
  3. Bug 2 + Bug 3 — Half-page logic & no missed-day penalty

  Root cause (Bug 2):
  // Line 102 — progressController.js
  const targetNewPages = dailyNewPages < 1 ? 1 : Math.ceil(dailyNewPages);
  // → 0.5/day always becomes 1 page/day

  Root cause (Bug 3): Same line — if you miss 2 days at 1 page/day, you still
  only get Math.ceil(1) = 1 page next day, so doubling isn't actually happening.
   But if review backlog accumulates, the slice(0, remainingReviews) cap handles
   it. The real issue is 0.5 users always get 1 page/day with no alternation.

  Fix (solves both): Replace the single line with a per-day allocation
  calculation:

  const planStart = user.planStartDate || user.createdAt;
  const msPerDay = 86400000;
  const daysPassed = Math.floor((Date.now() - new Date(planStart)) / msPerDay);

  // How many pages "should" have been assigned by end of today vs end of 
  yesterday
  const assignedThroughToday    = Math.ceil(user.dailyNewPages * (daysPassed +
  1));
  const assignedThroughYesterday = Math.ceil(user.dailyNewPages * daysPassed);
  const targetNewPages = assignedThroughToday - assignedThroughYesterday; // 0 
  or 1

  Examples:
  - 0.5/day: day 0→1, day 1→0, day 2→1, day 3→0 (alternates — 1 page every 2
  days)
  - 1/day: always 1
  - 2/day: always 2

  Missed days do NOT accumulate — today's allocation is always the same
  regardless of what happened yesterday.

  Also update completeOnboarding to set planStartDate: new Date() on the user.

  ---
  4. Feature 1 — Dynamic review system

  Root cause: const dailyReviewTarget = 3; — hardcoded, ignores how much is
  memorized.

  Fix: Replace with intensity-based cycle:

  ┌───────────┬─────────┬───────────────────────────┐
  │ Intensity │  Cycle  │ dailyReviewTarget formula │
  ├───────────┼─────────┼───────────────────────────┤
  │ light     │ 14 days │ ceil(totalMemorized / 14) │
  ├───────────┼─────────┼───────────────────────────┤
  │ standard  │ 10 days │ ceil(totalMemorized / 10) │
  ├───────────┼─────────┼───────────────────────────┤
  │ strong    │ 7 days  │ ceil(totalMemorized / 7)  │
  └───────────┴─────────┴───────────────────────────┘

  Minimum floor of 3 so brand-new users don't get 0 review pages.

  Examples at standard:
  - 7 Juz (~140 pages): 140/10 = 14 pages/day
  - 14 Juz (~280 pages): 280/10 = 28 pages/day
  - 30 Juz (604 pages): 604/10 = 61 pages/day (Hafiz mode)

  The per-Juz tiers you specified (7-day for ≤14 Juz, 10-day for ≥15 Juz) map
  naturally to strong vs standard intensity — users can self-select.

  Also update PUT /api/auth/profile to accept reviewIntensity and GET 
  /api/auth/me to return it.

  ---
  5. Feature 2 — Off days

  User model: offDays: [Number] (e.g. [5, 6] = Friday + Saturday).

  In getTodayTasks: If today's day-of-week (UTC) is in offDays, return early:
  { "isOffDay": true, "newPages": [], "reviewPages": [], "stats": { ... } }

  Streak maintenance: Currently the streak breaks if lastActiveDate isn't
  yesterday. With off days, we need to tolerate gaps that are entirely off days.
   Fix in markPageComplete:
  - Count actual "active days" (non-off days) between lastActiveDate and today
  - If 0 active days missed → streak continues
  - If ≥1 active day missed → streak resets to 1

  In PUT /api/auth/profile: Accept offDays array (validate: each element 0–6,
  max 2 days).

  ---
  6. Feature 3 — Hafiz mode

  Trigger: totalMemorized === 604

  When in Hafiz mode in getTodayTasks:
  - newPages = [], targetNewPages = 0
  - Review target uses the full intensity formula: ceil(604 / cycleDays) → 44–87
   pages/day depending on intensity
  - Return isHafiz: true in the response stats block

  The completion flag todayComplete already accounts for this partially — this
  makes it explicit and adds the Hafiz flag.

  ---
  Summary of all file changes

  ┌──────────────────────────────────────────┬───────────────────────────────┐
  │                   File                   │            Changes            │
  ├──────────────────────────────────────────┼───────────────────────────────┤

※ recap: Planning backend logic improvements for the Quran Tracker graduation project — bugs and new features covering review scheduling, off days, and half-page daily goals. Waiting for approval to start implementing.

❯ The plan looks good overall. Two adjustments before you start:                
                                                                                
  1. Add a MAX CAP for daily review pages:                                      
     - Maximum 40 pages/day (roughly 2 Juz) for non-Hafiz users                 
     - Maximum 60 pages/day (roughly 3 Juz) for Hafiz users                     
     - So the formula becomes:                                                  
       min(ceil(totalMemorized / cycleDays), maxCap)                            
                                                                                
     This prevents overwhelming users who memorized a lot                       
     but chose "light" intensity expecting a relaxed schedule.                  
                                                                                
  2. For Hafiz mode, give the user a choice:                                    
     - Light: ~2 Juz/day (finish review in 15 days)                             
     - Standard: ~3 Juz/day (finish in 10 days)                                 
     - Strong: ~4-5 Juz/day (finish in 7 days)                                  
     These are common Hafiz review schedules.                                   
                                                                                
  3. Also add a minimum floor:                                                  
     - If user has less than 3 memorized pages,                                 
       review target = total memorized pages (review everything)                
                                                                                
  Everything else is approved. Start implementing.

  What was implemented

  embedded in getTodayTasks stats as estimatedDays.
  - Bug 2 + 3 — computeTodayNewPageTarget: uses planStartDate + day index to
  compute today's allocation. For 0.5/day: alternates 1,0,1,0. Missed days never
   accumulate.

  ceil(memorized/cycleDays), capped at 40 for non-Hafiz, dedicated schedule for
  Hafiz (40/60/87).

  tolerates gaps that are entirely off days.
  - Feature 3 — Hafiz mode: isHafiz flag in every response, review-only mode
  with intensity-based targets (40/60/87 pages/day).
  - Performance — replaced N individual QuranMetadata.findOne calls with one
  batched $in query via getMetadataMap.

  server/routes/progressRoutes.js
  - Added GET /api/progress/estimate

  server/controllers/authController.js
  - getMe now returns reviewIntensity, offDays
  - updateProfile now accepts and validates reviewIntensity and offDays;
  updateProfile response also returns the new fields

  added.

   the card. Card has thin gradient top border. Inputs use #f0f3ff bg. Removed
  "remember me". Button is plain "Log In".

  Register — Radial gradient + pattern bg. Book icon inside card. All labels
  uppercase. Inputs #f0f3ff. Button "Create Account →".

  Dashboard — #FFFDF5 + sacred pattern. Welcome card + 2×2 stats bento grid (not
   4-card row). Decorative star in welcome card background. Two-column task
  layout (New | Review side-by-side). Task cards have colored left border
  (green/amber). Off-day shows centered rest-day card with reflection buttons.
  Tip of Day is collapsible.

  Onboarding step 2 — Single amber progress bar. Juz grid in a bordered card
  with "Select All ✓✓" link. Daily goal cards are large centered buttons with no
   label tabs. Estimate section uses #f0f3ff bg with flag icon.

  Settings — Left sidebar (Memorization Plan | Appearance). Prior Memorization
  shows live Juz chips fetched from API. Intensity uses radio-style cards
  ("Intensive" label). Rest days use checkboxes (Mon–Sun). "Save Changes"
  appears only when changes are pending.
